        <script>
            (function(){
                const root=document.getElementById("asm-faq-reviews");
                    if(!root)return;

                const btns=root.querySelectorAll(".asm-acc-item");
                    btns.forEach(btn=>{
                    btn.addEventListener("click",()=>{
                        const panel=btn.nextElementSibling;
                        const open=btn.getAttribute("aria-expanded")==="true";

                    btns.forEach(b=>{
                    b.setAttribute("aria-expanded","false");
                        if(b.nextElementSibling)b.nextElementSibling.hidden=true;
                    b.querySelector(".asm-icon").textContent="+";
                });

                if(!open){
                    btn.setAttribute("aria-expanded","true");
                    panel.hidden=false;
                    btn.querySelector(".asm-icon").textContent="–";
                    }
                });
            });
        })();
        </script>

        productImageSwapQueue.gallery,
